- **arxiv-analysis**: Analyze arXiv research papers and explain them in accessible terms. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/tools/arxiv-analysis) · ★67 · 2 copies)
- **arxiv-analyze**: Fetch and analyze an arXiv paper via tiered fallback (markdown -&gt; arXiv HTML -&gt; ar5iv -&gt; PDF). Rate-limited for arxiv2md (28 req/min, deterministic). Produces a structured summary with citation, problem, key claims, method, results, limitations. Use when the user says "analyze arxiv paper", "summarize arxiv", "read paper", "what does this paper say", or provides an arXiv ID/URL to analyze. ([fern-works/greenline](https://github.com/fern-works/greenline/tree/HEAD/corpus/upstream/ai-skill-arxiv/bbbe55b4c91ecd0555b819e3f547a7a3f379c8f5/arxiv-analyze))
- **arxiv-api**: Search and retrieve preprints from the arXiv open-access repository ([brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/43-wentorai-research-plugins/skills/literature/search/arxiv-api) · ★5.1k · 10 copies)
- **arxiv-article**: Downloads an arXiv HTML article, converts it to markdown with pandoc, archives it, and returns a structured summary. Use when processing a single arXiv paper given its HTML URL (e.g., https://arxiv.org/html/2504.12345). Called by the arxiv-catchup skill but can also be invoked directly. ([tomzx/agents](https://github.com/tomzx/agents/tree/HEAD/skills/arxiv-article) · ★17)
- **arxiv-audit**: Monthly re-audit of arXiv (cs.CR, cs.LG) for new papers on label delay, labelling or analyst budget, and conformal or drift monitoring in malware detection, then triage into docs/related_work_addendum.md. Use it when the researcher asks for the arXiv audit, the related-work refresh or "what is new in the literature", when an "arXiv audit" issue is opened by the monthly workflow, or when a prompt says TASK = arxiv-audit, even if they do not name the skill. ([Yathindra25KB/driftwatch-lifetime](https://github.com/Yathindra25KB/driftwatch-lifetime/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/arxiv-audit))
- **arxiv-automation**: Search and monitor arXiv papers. Query by topic, author, or category. Track new papers, download PDFs, and summarize abstracts for research workflows. ([aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team](https://github.com/aAAaqwq/AGI-Super-Team/tree/HEAD/skills/arxiv-automation) · 2 copies)
- **arxiv-batch-reporter**: Build the final collection report in two steps: model writes a report template, then script injects each paper's Brief Conclusion and abs URL from summary.md by arXiv-id placeholders. ([modbender/skill-library-mcp](https://github.com/modbender/skill-library-mcp/tree/HEAD/data/arxiv-batch-reporter) · ★21 · 5 copies)
- **arxiv-batch-reporting**: Batch search and report generation from arXiv preprint repository ([brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/43-wentorai-research-plugins/skills/literature/search/arxiv-batch-reporting) · ★5.1k · 9 copies)
- **arxiv-bridge**: Upstream arXiv paper discovery layer for scientific/academic queries. Bridges local corpus retrieval with live Semantic Scholar and arXiv Atom APIs. Use when the local CSV/pgvector corpus may not have the most relevant recent papers, or when the user's query warrants fresh upstream discovery. ([thistleknot/skills](https://github.com/thistleknot/skills/tree/HEAD/arxiv-bridge))
- **arxiv-caixinyu2017-star-xixi**: This page used to hold an archived copy of this skill. The archive now lives in the registry's data repository. ([majiayu000/claude-skill-registry](https://github.com/majiayu000/claude-skill-registry/tree/HEAD/skills/bash/arxiv-caixinyu2017-star-xixi) · ★745)
- **arxiv-catchup**: Fetches new arXiv cs.AI papers published since the last processed date, processes each paper's HTML version via the arxiv-article skill, and advances the date checkpoint. - **arxiv-categorical-ai**: Systematic analysis patterns for categorical AI papers (Gavranović, de Wynter, Bradley, Zhang). ([manutej/meta-prompting-plugin](https://github.com/manutej/meta-prompting-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/arxiv-categorical-ai))
- **arxiv-cite-integrity**: Verify papers and citations before use. Blocks fabricated refs, wrong IDs, invented numbers. Use when citing arXiv or summarizing papers. ([linusc9516/dotfiles](https://github.com/linusc9516/dotfiles/tree/HEAD/agents/skills/arxiv-cite-integrity))
- **arxiv-claim-judge**: Stateless per-paper claim judge. Use when arxiv-scout's Phase 3.6 judge gate spawns it (Stage 1). Checks claim-vs-evidence consistency in the PDF and grades claim_strength; returns raw pass | flag | inconclusive + bold | solid | mediocre — the caller owns keep/drop. ([RoyYu0509/auto-research-monorepo](https://github.com/RoyYu0509/auto-research-monorepo/tree/HEAD/plugins/arxiv-recommender-ar/skills/arxiv-claim-judge))
- **arxiv-claim-judge**: Stateless per-paper claim judge. Use when arxiv-scout's Phase 3.6 judge gate spawns it (Stage 1). From ONE full-text read it checks claim-vs-evidence consistency, grades claim_strength, and grades how much follow-up room the paper leaves (extensibility_verdict); returns raw verdicts only — the caller owns keep/drop. ([RoyYu0509/ideation-claude](https://github.com/RoyYu0509/ideation-claude/tree/HEAD/plugins/arxiv-recommender-ar/skills/arxiv-claim-judge))
- **arxiv-clean-project**: Prepare a LaTeX/Overleaf project for arXiv submission with google-research/arxiv-latex-cleaner — strip source comments, delete draft macros (\todo, \note, review environments), and optionally shrink oversized figures to fit arXiv's 50MB limit. Produces either a separate submission-ready copy (the tool's native mode) or applies the cleaning back into the project in place. Use when the user asks to "clean", "arxiv-clean", "prepare for arXiv/submission", "strip comments/todos", or "make a submission copy" of an Overleaf/LaTeX project. Operates on projects served by the web-latex-mcp MCP server.… ([elias-ramzi/WebLatexMCP](https://github.com/elias-ramzi/WebLatexMCP/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/arxiv-clean-project) · ★16)

Returns the raw verdict JSON; the caller owns all convergence decisions. - **arxiv-refine**: Use when arxiv-scout's Phase 5 Refine spawns it to turn one ideate idea into a research plan — novelty check, Section 0 significance floor, four rigor-tiered sections (Claim/Proposition/Baselines/Validation), priority issues, and a /research seed. - **arxiv-refute**: Mechanism-refutation hunter for the arxiv-ideation verified-kill loop. WebSearch is open at THIS leaf only. Returns clear|scooped + the killing hit. ([RoyYu0509/ideation-claude](https://github.com/RoyYu0509/ideation-claude/tree/HEAD/plugins/arxiv-recommender-ar/skills/arxiv-refute))
